- `daynight`: Day and Night — a 32×32 torus where a dead cell is born with 3, 6, 7 or 8 live neighbours and a live cell stays with 3, 4, 6, 7 or 8, a random soup of 50% density warmed up for 24 generations, the tile showing four consecutive generations (2×2 panels of 32×32 cells), a pale yellow and dark blue-grey camouflage; the tests check only through sampler read-backs that each of the last three panels is exactly the next generation of the panel before it by this rule, with the ordinary Life rule as a control; they do not show that the rule's name is right (from memory), that the soup is uniformly random or that the warm-up is right; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. (Different from the existing `gameoflife` and `elementaryautomata`.) Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1065 kinds, 1066 gallery choices including plain.

- `hamiltoncycle`: Hamiltonian cycle — a random Hamiltonian cycle of a 32×32 grid (a closed path through every cell exactly once): a random spanning tree of a 16×16 coarse grid is thickened, and a thick lime-green line winds like a labyrinth; the tests check only through sampler read-backs that all 1,024 cell centres are lit, that every cell is joined to exactly two neighbours and that walking along the joins visits exactly 1,024 cells and returns to the start; this is not shown for every seed (only the six test seeds and 60 probe seeds were checked) and the construction was written from memory without looking it up; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. (The closest existing kinds are `moorecurve` and `perfectmaze`; this one is a random 32×32 Hamiltonian cycle.) Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1064 kinds, 1065 gallery choices including plain.

- `paleyhadamard`: Paley Hadamard — a Hadamard matrix from Paley's first construction, a prime q (43, 47, 59, 67, 71 or 79, picked by the seed) giving order q + 1, with row 0 all +1, column 0 below it all −1 and the body the quadratic character of (i − j), +1 bright gold and −1 dark blue-grey, with diagonal bands and a cross along row 0 and column 0; the tests check only through sampler read-backs, pair by pair from the definition, that any two rows are orthogonal, and that flipping an entry or reading at a wrong order breaks the orthogonality; they do not show the theorem behind Paley's construction or that every seed falls on one of the six orders; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. (Different from the existing `hadamardmatrix` and `paleytable`.) Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1063 kinds, 1064 gallery choices including plain.

- `cayleytable`: Cayley table — the multiplication table of a group of order 16, the seed picking one of Z16, Z8×Z2, Z4×Z4, Z4×Z2×Z2, Z2⁴ and the dihedral group of the octagon, 16×16 rose-toned tiles with element 0 as the identity; the tests check only through sampler read-backs that it is a Latin square with the identity and associative for all 4,096 triples, and identify the six groups by whether the table is symmetric, the number of elements squaring to the identity and the largest order; the expected invariants were worked out by hand and not checked against the literature, and the tables are not shown to be cell for cell the textbook ones; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. (Different from the existing `xortable`, `modulartable` and `paleytable`.) Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1062 kinds, 1063 gallery choices including plain.

- `sortingnetwork`: Sorting network — a schematic of a sorting network for 16 wires, with each comparator drawn as a vertical link between two wires with a bright dot at each end; the seed picks Batcher's odd-even merge sort (63 comparators), the odd-even transposition sort (120) or the bitonic sort in standard form (80), a green circuit pattern; the tests check only through sampler read-backs: after reading the dots, links and wires they apply the read-back network to all 65,536 inputs of 0s and 1s (the 0-1 principle) to check that it really sorts, and check that leaving out a comparator stops it sorting; they do not show that nothing else is drawn or that the networks are optimal; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1061 kinds, 1062 gallery choices including plain.

- `relativeneighborhood`: Relative neighbourhood graph — the relative neighbourhood graph of a Poisson-disk point set on the torus (minimum distance 0.085, about 90 points): two points are joined when no third point is closer to both than they are to each other, nodes brighter and edges thinner, a pink network sparser than the Gabriel graph and with a few more cycles than the minimum spanning tree; the tests check only through sampler read-backs: after reading the node centres they use a classification written separately in the test from the definition and check whether five sample points along the segment are all lit, for pairs that are clearly edges and for pairs that are clearly not (chain-like pairs excluded); pairs within 0.003 are not judged and connectivity and planarity are not shown; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1060 kinds, 1061 gallery choices including plain.

- `euclideanmst`: Euclidean MST — the minimum spanning tree (Kruskal, pairs at most 0.25 apart) of a Poisson-disk point set on the torus (minimum distance 0.085, about 90 points), nodes brighter and edges thinner, an amber tree-like network like constellation lines; the tests check only through sampler read-backs: after reading the node centres they use a criterion different from the sampler's (the cycle property, with a margin of 0.003) and check whether five sample points along the segment are all lit, for pairs that are clearly in the tree and for pairs that are clearly not (chain-like pairs excluded); unjudged pairs and connectivity are not shown; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. (It differs from `gabrielgraph` in that its edges are a much smaller subgraph of the Gabriel graph.) Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1059 kinds, 1060 gallery choices including plain.

- `gabrielgraph`: Gabriel graph — the Gabriel graph of a Poisson-disk point set on the torus (minimum distance 0.085, about 90 points): two points are joined when no third point lies inside the disc that has the segment between them as its diameter; nodes are brighter and edges thinner, a cyan network; the tests check only through sampler read-backs: after reading the node centres they use a classification written separately in the test from the definition and check whether five sample points along the segment are all lit, for pairs that are clearly edges and for pairs that are clearly not (chain-like pairs excluded); ambiguous pairs are not judged and planarity and connectivity are not shown; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. (It differs from the existing `poissondisk` in that it also draws the edges.) Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1058 kinds, 1059 gallery choices including plain.

- `schelling`: Schelling segregation — a 48×48 torus with about 10% empty cells and two kinds of resident of about 45% each; a resident is happy when at least 40% of its 8 neighbours that are occupied are of its own kind, and unhappy residents move in turn to the first empty cell where they would be happy (at most 60 sweeps), forming amber and brown patches like camouflage with dark plum empty cells; the tests check only through sampler read-backs, with a happiness test written separately in the test from the definition, that every resident is happy and none could be made happy by an empty cell; they do not show that the state came from one particular order of moves or that every seed converges; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; the construction time has only a single uncontrolled measurement. Current catalog: 1057 kinds, 1058 gallery choices including plain.

- `josephus`: Josephus — a 64×64 table whose cell in row n−1 and column k−1 shows which eighth of the circle the survivor stands in when n people stand in a circle and every k-th is eliminated (7 steps from deep navy to gold), the seed shifting it around the torus; the tests recompute the whole table by actually playing the elimination out (the sampler uses the recurrence) and cross-check the closed forms for k = 1 and k = 2 and the 31st survivor of 41 people with every third eliminated; they show no statistical property of the irregular regions; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. - `abundantnumbers`: Abundant numbers — 48×48 rounded-square tiles where the tile for n (1 to 2304) is toned in three classes by comparing σ(n) with 2n: deficient numbers (1,732) dim, abundant numbers (569) brighter and perfect numbers (6, 28, 496) brightest, with multiples of 12 and 6 forming bright vertical bars and the seed moving the whole table around the torus; the tiles match a classification table computed independently by trial division in the tests tile by tile; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.3 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0. Current catalog: 984 kinds, 985 gallery choices including plain.

- `xortable`: XOR table — 64×64 rounded-square tiles whose tone is proportional to row XOR col (the main diagonal darkest, brighter lime as the value grows, forming nested Sierpinski-like blocks, the XOR texture), with the seed moving the table around the torus; the tiles match a table written independently in the tests tile by tile; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.3 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0. Current catalog: 983 kinds, 984 gallery choices including plain.

- `paleytable`: Paley table — 61×61 rounded-square tiles where tile (row, col) is the Legendre symbol of col − row modulo the prime 61 (diagonal middle tone, quadratic residues bright rose pink, non-residues dark plum); the table depends only on col − row so it shows diagonal bands, and the seed moves it around the torus; the tiles match a table written independently in the tests tile by tile; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.3 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0. Current catalog: 982 kinds, 983 gallery choices including plain.

- `hadamardmatrix`: Hadamard matrix — 64×64 rounded-square tiles where tile (row, col) is the matching entry of the Sylvester Hadamard matrix (+1 bright cool white when the number of 1 bits in row AND col is even, −1 dark blue-grey when odd), with the seed moving the whole table around the torus; the tiles match a matrix written independently in the tests tile by tile; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared, and the nested block structure is not recognisable by eye in the preview. - `radarscope`: radar-scope display schematics: one circular scope per tile on a dark green-black ground, with 5 evenly spaced range rings, 24 bearing spokes every 15° (every 6th heavier), a bright sweep arm with a quadratically fading phosphor afterglow, 6 random blips and 192 scanlines. A UI-style schematic without text or scale marks; the sweep arm is fixed and does not move over time. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.0004 UV-relative relief.
- `hazardstripes`: worn hazard-warning-stripe schematics: 45° yellow-and-black stripes (8 per tile) over steel, with the paint chipped by noise chips, scratches along the stripes and an edge bias, exposing neutral-gray bare steel through the existing mortar mix; the metallic M mask and the mortar weight are the same wear value (M1 = bare steel, M0 = paint). Not the color or proportion mandated by any standard, without rust, welds or rivets. Defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.0032 UV-relative relief, metallic factor 1.
- `kiwislices`: kiwi-fruit slice schematics: one round slice per 2×2 cell with green flesh and 64 radial fibers, a pale yellow-green core, two rings of dark oval seeds (20 inner and 28 outer, offset by half a pitch) and a dark-brown fuzzy skin ring, with dark gaps between slices. An authored simplified schematic, not a measurement of any cultivar. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.25 m nominal tile, 0.0018 UV-relative relief.
- `mandalaflower`: mandala-flower schematics: one flower centered per tile (the seed picks the rotation) with, from the outside in, 12 lotus petals, a ring of 36 dots, 24 outer petals, 12 inner petals and a central hub, each layer filled and outlined, later layers higher. A simplified schematic, not a mandala of any religion or tradition. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.0014 UV-relative relief.
- `needlepoint`: needlepoint-tapestry schematics: 48×48 half-cross stitches per tile slanted along (1, 1) with a two-ply ripple and dark canvas gaps between them; color from a 12×12-stitch motif repeated 4×4 per tile (concentric diamond rings plus a bright cross accent, mirror-symmetric in both axes, with per-stitch tone jitter). A simplified schematic, not a particular stitch or design. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.0026 UV-relative relief.
- `dicefaces`: dice-face schematics: a 4×4 field of rounded ivory faces (domed bevel, dark gaps) each carrying 1–6 hemispherical recessed pips on a 3×3 lattice in the standard point-symmetric layouts (1 center, 2 diagonal, 3 diagonal plus center, 4 corners, 5 corners plus center, 6 two rows of three), with each face randomly turned 90° and mirrored. Whole-die properties such as opposite faces summing to 7 are not modeled. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.0034 UV-relative relief.
- `cinderblockwall`: concrete-masonry-unit wall: 8 courses × 4 blocks per tile in a half-block running bond at 2:1 blocks, recessed joints using the existing mortar channel; block faces carry per-block tone shifts, cloudy mottle, aggregate grain, chamfers and about 940 small air voids per tile. Hollow cores, efflorescence and paint are not modeled and no particular CMU specification is claimed. M0; defaults 1.6 m nominal tile, 0.0038 UV-relative relief.
- `azulejotiles`: Portuguese-style painted ceramic tiles: a 4×4 grid of identical cobalt-on-white glazed tiles per tile with recessed grout using the existing mortar channel; each tile has a four-lobed central rosette, a thin ring and quarter-disc corner motifs (joining into full circles where four tiles meet) with full square symmetry, brush-stroke blue tone, faint craquelure and bevels. Not a particular historical design, and all tiles are identical. M0; defaults 0.8 m nominal tile, 0.0022 UV-relative relief.
- `tumblingblocks`: tumbling-blocks (isometric cube) patchwork: a 7-column × 8-row hexagonal lattice per tile with each hexagon split into a light top, mid right and dark left rhombus, thin seams between faces, per-block tone jitter and woven-fabric grain. Light and dark faces are baked tone that does not follow light direction; the square UV stretches the lattice about 1% non-uniformly. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.0018 UV-relative relief.
- `kilimflatweave`: kilim flatweave rug: 48×48 weave cells per tile in four 12-row bands (stepped diamonds, stepped zigzag, horizontal stripes and crosses) with four tone steps (indigo, madder red, saffron, undyed white), per-cell dye jitter, horizontal weft ribs and vertical slits where colors change. A simplified schematic that claims no particular tribe or region; the tones are brightness, not hue. M0, mortar 0; defaults 1.2 m nominal tile, 0.0022 UV-relative relief.
- `ean13barcodes`: EAN-13 barcode labels: 4 labels per tile generated from the published encoding tables (guards 101 / 01010 / 101, six L or G codes, six R codes and a correct check digit, 9 modules of quiet zone each side); digits derive from the seed and row, are not registered product numbers and no readable text is drawn. Print quality is not measured and no physical scanner was used. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.35 m nominal tile, 0.0012 UV-relative relief.
- `morsetape`: Morse-code paper tape: 10 lines per tile, 64 units each, every line spelling one hash-chosen word from ten (SOS, XYZ, CAT, PLAY, HELLO, WORLD, LIGHT, ENGINE, SIGNAL, TEXTURE) in standard International Morse timing (dot 1, dash 3, mark gap 1, letter gap 3), with round-ended capsule marks, a faint guide line and paper fibers. No sprocket holes and a single word per line. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.0016 UV-relative relief.
- `sandripples`: wind-formed sand ripples: an asymmetric ripple train on the integer wave vector (18, 7) (gentle windward face over 72% of the phase, steep lee face over 28%) with crests bent by low-frequency noise; lighter windward and darker lee faces, grain relief and mica specks. No crest bifurcations, dune-scale forms or wet sand. M0; defaults 1 m nominal tile, 0.006 UV-relative relief.
- `frostpolygons`: arctic patterned ground: 8×8 jittered Voronoi polygons per tile with slightly lower silt centers, a raised stony rim holding round pebbles, and narrow deep ice-wedge troughs between polygons. Borders approximate the gap between the two nearest seeds; ice-wedge profiles, freeze–thaw cycles, moss and snow are not modeled. M0; defaults 1.5 m nominal tile, 0.012 UV-relative relief.
- `datamatrixcodes`: Data Matrix 2D-code labels: 3×3 ECC 200-style symbols of 18×18 modules per tile (solid L finder on the left column and bottom row, alternating clock track on the top row and right column, 1-module quiet zone) with a random-bit data region. Not valid ECC 200 codewords and not scannable. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.0012 UV-relative relief.
- `sudokugrid`: Sudoku grid: one 9×9 board per tile with thin cell lines and thick 3×3 box lines, each cell showing a clue in a 3×5 pixel font with about 42% probability; the clues come from a hidden complete solution built by rule-preserving permutations, so no row, column or box repeats. Unique solutions and difficulty are not guaranteed. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.25 m nominal tile, 0.0014 UV-relative relief.
- `measuringtape`: measuring tape: a 160 mm square tile of 8 yellow strips with a short tick every 1 mm, a medium tick every 5 mm and a long tick every 10 mm, and the centimeter number 0–15 in a 3×5 pixel font right of each centimeter tick. A repeating 0–15 cm segment with no inch scale or metal-tape curvature. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.16 m nominal tile, 0.0018 UV-relative relief.
- `calendarpage`: calendar page: 7 columns × 6 rows of month cells per tile, each with its date in a 3×5 pixel font and darker weekend columns; each seed picks a start column (0–6) and length (28–31 days), numbers rising consecutively in reading order with the rest blank. No weekday header or month name and no real month implied. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.0014 UV-relative relief.
- `fretboard`: guitar fretboard: one octave (nut to the 12th fret) of dark rosewood per tile, with frets at twelve-tone equal-temperament positions (fret n at 1 − 2^(−n/12), normalized by the 12th fret onto u = 1, each gap 2^(−1/12) of the previous), six strings thickening from treble to bass, pearl dots at frets 3, 5, 7 and 9 and a double dot at the 12th. The scale is normalized, and neither fretboard radius nor metal reflections are modeled. M0; defaults 0.32 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `flipdotdisplay`: flip-dot display: 48×48 discs per tile on a black panel, lit ones yellow (glossier, slightly raised, with a highlight and pivot mark) and unlit ones dark gray-black, forming a 12-column × 8-row grid of seed-chosen 0–9 digits (3×5 pixel font with a one-dot margin right and below). No flip animation or bistable mechanism, and the digits are a random sequence. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.003 UV-relative relief.
- `minesweeperboard`: Minesweeper board: one 24×24 torus board per tile with 16% hidden mines (not drawn), flood-opened from 7 safe zero cells; opened cells are flat and show the adjacent-mine count 1–8 in a 3×5 pixel font, unopened cells are raised buttons. No flags or exploded mines, and adjacency wraps at the tile boundary. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.003 UV-relative relief.
- `tetrisstack`: Tetris-style stack: one 16×16 well per tile with randomly placed rotations of the seven tetrominoes (I, O, T, S, Z, J, L), each kind in its own tone with bevels, a highlight and dark outlines, and unoccupied cells as dark pits. No gravity or line-clear rules and not a real game position. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.0035 UV-relative relief.
- `battleshipgrid`: Battleship board: one 10×10 board per tile with blue-gray water and grid lines, a straight-line fleet of 5, 4, 3, 3 and 2 cells (no two ships touching, even at corners), and about 32% of cells shot — dark pegs for hits and near-white pegs for misses. No coordinate labels or ship shapes, and the shot record is random. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.003 UV-relative relief.
- `mondriancomposition`: Mondrian-style composition: one recursively partitioned rectangle composition per tile (split lines span only the rectangle they divide, with a black frame at the tile edge), rectangles filled with one of six tones (about half white). No red, blue and yellow (brightness only, so grayscale) and not a copy of any work. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.0016 UV-relative relief.
- `lissajousgrid`: Lissajous-figure table: a 4×4 oscilloscope-style table per tile where cell (column, row) draws x = A·sin(a·t + π/2), y = A·sin(b·t) with a = column + 1 and b = row + 1 (a 160-segment polyline), as bright green lines with a glow and a faint cell frame. Fixed phase, no screen curvature or scan lines; every sample checks 160 segments, so it is costlier (a one-off local Bun run took about 155 ms for 512², not a benchmark). M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.002 UV-relative relief.
- `zoneplates`: Fresnel zone plates: a 2×2 array of plates per tile with 14 zones each, zone k ending at radius R·√(k/N) (equal-area zones), even zones open and odd zones opaque, with a retaining ring and a mid-gray backing. Only the radius-law geometry is drawn; diffraction and focus are not simulated. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.2 m nominal tile, 0.0022 UV-relative relief.
- `backgammonboard`: backgammon board: one board per tile with 6 alternating-tone triangular points on each side of a central bar and 15 checkers per side placed at random but legally on the 24 points (at most 5 per point, one color each). Not the standard opening; no dice, doubling cube or checkers on the bar. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.003 UV-relative relief.
- `jigsawpuzzle`: jigsaw puzzle: a 6×6-piece torus puzzle per tile with one round bulb per edge (the owning side chosen by the seed, fitting into the other so neighbors always interlock), thin cut lines with bevels, a low-frequency cloudy picture and per-piece tone variation. Bulbs are circles on straight necks, not a real die-cut; every sample runs roughly 40 ownership tests, so it is costlier (a one-off local Bun run took about 226 ms for 512², not a benchmark). M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.0026 UV-relative relief.
- `ramennoodles`: ramen noodles: 22 wavy strands per tile (k = 3, 4, 5, golden-ratio phases) layered from top to bottom with a round tube profile (bright center, darker edges) and about 7% dark broth showing between them. No curled noodles, breaks or toppings, and wave distance is a first-order approximation. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.01 UV-relative relief.
- `connectfourboard`: Connect Four board: one 7×6 board plus a base tray row per tile, with round holes in a blue panel and discs (first player mid-tone, second near-white) in a randomly simulated legal position (gravity stacks, the first player has equal or one more disc, nobody has four in a row). The colors are brightness, not real red and yellow. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `bingocard`: bingo card: 6×6 cells per tile, with a bingo card in the left 5 columns × 6 rows — a pale-gray "BINGO" header, distinct numbers per column in the standard ranges (1–15, 16–30, 31–45, 46–60, 61–75) in a 3×5 pixel font, a free center and about 25% daub circles — and a blank margin column on the right. The daubs are random and do not represent a call state. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.0014 UV-relative relief.
- `pegsolitaire`: peg solitaire: one 7×7 English cross board per tile (33 holes, solid-wood corners) with dark empty holes and light round-headed pegs; the position is made by playing 8 moves backwards from the solved end state (one peg in the center), so it can always be solved to a single peg. Only the 33-hole English board is drawn and the puzzle is not guaranteed to be representative. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.35 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `xylophonebars`: xylophone bars: 12 side-by-side bars per tile (the twelve semitones), each shortened by 2^(−1/24) per step by tuning law (2^(−1/2) per octave), with suspension holes at the 22.4% node points joined by a thin cord, on dark felt. Bar thickness, undercut cross-section and resonator tubes are not modeled, and the cord snaps back at the tile seam. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.0035 UV-relative relief.
- `sliderulescale`: slide-rule C scales: 8 ivory logarithmic scales per tile, one decade across the tile width, graduation x drawn at u = log10(x) (150 ticks at three lengths, with 3×5 digits 1 to 9 beside the integers). Only a single decade of the C scale is drawn — no cursor and no other scales. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.25 m nominal tile, 0.002 UV-relative relief.
- `spirographrosettes`: spirograph rosettes: one hypotrochoid per quadrant (ring/gear ratios 5/3, 8/3, 7/2, 9/4 giving 5, 8, 7 and 9 lobes) with the pen distance varying by seed and all lobe tips on one outer circle; the curves are stamped into a 640×640 ink mask when the sampler is created and each sample is a bilinear lookup. Only the four fixed ratios are drawn. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.0012 UV-relative relief.
- `juliacells`: Julia-set cells: one filled Julia set z → z² + c per quadrant (real c of about −0.4/−0.75/−1.0/−1.25, jittered by the seed); the view radius equals the escape radius so there is no seam between cells or at the tile repeat, and the outside glows by smooth escape count. Real c only, iteration cap 96, no Mandelbrot set. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.0035 UV-relative relief.
- `nonogrampuzzle`: nonogram (a solved sheet): a 12×12-cell tile whose bottom-right 8×8 is a randomly filled picture, with the run-length clues of every row and column printed as 3×5 dot-matrix digits in the 4 columns/rows to the left and above (computed from the same data, so always consistent) and heavier lines every five cells. A unique solution is not guaranteed. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.36 m nominal tile, 0.0016 UV-relative relief.
- `lightsoutboard`: Lights Out board: a 5×5 board per tile of rounded buttons, lit (warm yellow with a highlight) or unlit (dark brown), set in a dark frame; the lit pattern comes from pressing about 40% random buttons on the all-off board, so it is always solvable. Only the standard 5×5 static position is drawn. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.25 m nominal tile, 0.003 UV-relative relief.
- `weavingdraft`: weaving draft: a 16×16-cell sheet showing the threading, tie-up and treadling of a four-shaft, four-treadle loom and the 12×12 drawdown they determine (dark = warp float, light = weft float). Threading and treadling are random, not a traditional twill rule, and there is no yarn color. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.0018 UV-relative relief.
- `scrambledcubenet`: scrambled cube net: a 14×14-cell sheet with the cross-shaped net of a 3×3×3 cube in the middle (54 stickers, six brightnesses for the six faces), scrambled by 22 random quarter turns of outer layers so it is always a legal state (9 stickers per color, each of the 12 edges and 8 corners once, corner handedness preserved). Brightness only, not hue. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.0028 UV-relative relief.
- `graycodediscs`: Gray-code encoder discs: four optical encoder discs per tile (3/4/5/6 concentric tracks, 8 to 64 sectors) where track k is a bright clear slot when bit k of the sector's Gray code is 1 and a dark opaque block otherwise, so neighboring sectors differ in exactly one track; with rim ticks and a center shaft hole. Track counts are fixed and the seed only changes the mat grain. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.35 m nominal tile, 0.0028 UV-relative relief.
- `code39barcodes`: Code 39 barcodes: 6 rows of `*XXXXX*` per tile (the `*` delimiters around 5 seed-chosen characters from 0–9, A–Z, `-`, `.`), each character 9 elements of which 3 are wide (3:1), one narrow space between characters and quiet zones on both sides. The pattern table was typed from memory and decoded back independently with the structural rule described on Wikipedia; it was not scanned with a real reader, and there is no check digit, no $/+% and no human-readable text. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.0012 UV-relative relief.
- `amidakuji`: ghost-leg lottery: 8 vertical lines with 10 levels of random rungs per tile (never two touching on a level), the start numbers 1 to 8 on top and, at the bottom, the start that ends under each line (a permutation of the top row). Rungs sit at level centers and no winning path is marked. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.0016 UV-relative relief.
- `featherbarbs`: feather barbs: two feather vanes per tile whose bright shafts send barbs sweeping tailward to both sides (48 per tile at a fixed slope, each barb a different length for a ragged outer edge) with dark gaps between them. Only barbs and shaft are drawn — no barbules or curvature; the high-frequency stripes alias at small sizes. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.25 m nominal tile, 0.003 UV-relative relief.
- `dartboardsectors`: dartboard: one standard board per tile (bull and outer bull, 20 alternating black/cream sectors in the standard order 20, 1, 18, …, 5 clockwise from the top, each with single, treble and double areas, wire boundaries and a ring of upright dot-matrix numbers), dimensioned in millimetres and scaled. The overall diameter and the section count were confirmed from Wikipedia; the ring radii and number order were typed from memory (the order passes the adjacent-difference-sum-198 self-check); red/green are only greys. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.0028 UV-relative relief.
- `tictactoegames`: tic-tac-toe: four 3×3 boards per tile, each the position of a random game (X first, stopped at the first line, otherwise after 3 to 9 plies), so X has as many marks as O or one more and the two sides never both have a line. The line and move order are not marked, and there are no dividers between the four boards. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.002 UV-relative relief.
- `quasicrystalwaves`: quasicrystal waves: five plane waves with integer wave vectors ((6,0), (2,6), (−5,4), (−5,−4), (2,−6), roughly pentagon directions) summed and squashed through tanh into the brightness, with seed-chosen phases, exactly periodic so it repeats seamlessly. A periodic approximant, not a true quasicrystal (no Penrose-style aperiodicity). M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.003 UV-relative relief.
- `tallymarks`: tally marks: 8 ruled rows per tile, each with gate-style tally groups of five (four upright strokes and a strike, the last group possibly incomplete) and the total (3 to 49) printed at the right in 3×5 dot-matrix digits, always consistent with the stroke count. Perfect straight lines with no hand wobble, and not the East Asian 正 count. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.0016 UV-relative relief.
- `polargraphpaper`: polar graph paper: one centered chart per tile with 12 equally spaced concentric circles (5th, 10th and outermost heavier) and 36 radial lines every 10° (every 30° heavier, the four axes heaviest), with a blank disc at the center. No scale numbers; line widths are fixed and vanish at very low resolutions such as 32. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.0012 UV-relative relief.
- `chesterfieldtufting`: Chesterfield diamond-tufted leather: a round button at each of the 8 grid points of a 4×4 grid with an even index sum, creases pulled diagonally between neighboring buttons, and the diamonds between them puffed into cushions (height (sin·sin)^0.7). No button holes or stitches, and the hide texture is brightness noise. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.02 UV-relative relief.
- `louvershutter`: louvered shutter: two stiles, a central tilt rod and 10 tilted slats per tile (sloped faces bright at the top and dark toward the bottom, with a dark gap over the bottom 14%) and a pin on the rod at the middle of each slat. A simplified head-on view with no perspective or light showing through. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.012 UV-relative relief.
- `ledgersheet`: ledger sheet: 8 rows per tile, each with a row number, a right-aligned three-digit amount (100 to 999) and a running balance (the sum of the amounts so far) in 3×5 dot-matrix digits with column and row rules; every balance equals the previous balance plus the row's amount. No headings or currency signs. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.0014 UV-relative relief.
- `sundialplate`: sundial plate: one horizontal sundial face per tile (a bronze disc with 13 hour lines, the line h hours from noon at atan(sin φ·tan(15°h)) from the noon line, latitude φ chosen by the seed between 25° and 55°), three concentric rings and hour numerals alternating between two radii. Only the face — no 3D gnomon or shadow, no longitude correction or equation of time. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.003 UV-relative relief.
- `doubleslitfringes`: double-slit interference fringes: four panels per tile (slit separation / width r = 2, 3, 4, 5) with intensity I = cos²(πx/p)·sinc²(πx/(r·p)), so every r-th order is missing; an idealized far-field formula, not an experimental image. Mirror symmetric and seamlessly repeating. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.002 UV-relative relief.
- `fibonaccisquares`: Fibonacci squares and golden spiral: a 13 × 21 unit rectangle per tile filled exactly by seven squares of side 1, 1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, each with a quarter circle tangent to its neighbors at the shared end points, tracing the golden spiral (an approximation, not a true logarithmic spiral). The rectangle does not fill the tile and leaves margins. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.0016 UV-relative relief.
- `bluenoisemask`: blue-noise dither mask: 64×64 texels ranked 0 to 4095 by void-and-cluster on a torus (every rank used exactly once, so the histogram is exactly flat and low frequencies are suppressed), repeating seamlessly. Fixed at 64×64 and sampled nearest-neighbour (each texel is an 8×8 px block at 512 px). M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.2 m nominal tile, 0.002 UV-relative relief.
- `mastermindboard`: Mastermind board: 6 columns × 9 rows per tile — the top row is the revealed code (4 pegs, 6 colors), the 8 rows below are random guesses, each with a 2×2 block of feedback holes scored by the rules (exact = bright, partial = dark, filled in order). The guesses are random, not reasoned, and colors are only brightness. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.35 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `wordsearchgrid`: word search: a 12×12 letter grid per tile (only the 12 letters A C D E G I L M N O R T, as 3×5 dot-matrix capitals) with 5 words from a list of 15 placed at random in the eight directions and the rest filled with random letters. The list is not printed and the filler may spell other words. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.35 m nominal tile, 0.0014 UV-relative relief.
- `slidingpuzzle`: sliding (15-)puzzle: a 4×4 board per tile with 15 tiles numbered 1 to 15 in dot matrix and one sunken empty square, shuffled from the solved state by 240 seeded legal slides (always solvable). Not a uniformly random permutation and a few tiles may still be home. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `oghaminscription`: Ogham inscription: eight horizontal carved rows on rough grey stone, each with a stem line and a word of letters made of one to five strokes (below / above / diagonal / short notches on the stem), each row choosing one of 16 Old Irish tree names. The letter order is from memory and the layout is simplified to horizontal. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.006 UV-relative relief.
- `pascalmodulo`: Pascal's triangle modulo n: 2×2 triangles of 32 rows per tile, the quadrants holding residues modulo 2, 3, 4 and 5 (brightness is the residue; modulo 2 is the Sierpiński triangle), with the seed choosing which modulus goes where. Only 32 rows, and residue 0 is a darker square, not empty. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.003 UV-relative relief.
- `eightqueens`: eight queens: 2×2 chessboards of 8×8 per tile, each with eight non-attacking queens (one per row and column, none on a shared diagonal; round-body three-spike-crown silhouettes), each found by a seed-shuffled backtracking search. The solutions are not uniform over the 92 and can coincide by chance. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `hanoitowers`: Tower of Hanoi: 2×2 panels per tile, each with three pegs and six disks, showing in reading order the optimal 63-move solution after 0, 21, 42 and 63 moves; larger disks are wider and brighter. One fixed solution and move counts, the seed changes only the wood grain. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.006 UV-relative relief.
- `mayansums`: Maya addition: in each of four quadrants a sum a + b = c is carved on rough grey stone in base-20 numerals (20s digit above the units digit; shell = 0, dot = 1, bar = 5) with a and b picked by the seed from 10 to 199. A simplified drawing, not researched glyphs, and no Long Count. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.006 UV-relative relief.
- `sortingnetworks`: sorting networks: four 8-wire comparator diagrams per tile, each picked by the seed between Batcher's odd-even merge sort (19 comparators) and the bitonic sorter (24), one comparator per column. The 0-1 principle confirms the network read back sorts all 256 binary inputs. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.003 UV-relative relief.
- `knockoutbracket`: knockout bracket: a 16-team single-elimination tree per tile (round one is teams 1 to 16 shuffled by the seed, five columns of boxes up to the champion, each winner's number taken from one of the two boxes feeding it, results random). No scores or names, fixed at 16 teams. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `skyscraperpuzzles`: Skyscraper puzzles: 2×2 solved puzzles per tile (orders 4, 5, 6, 7): the middle n×n grid holds building heights (dot-matrix digits, 1 to n once in each row and column) and the ring around it holds the visible-building clues from each side. It shows the answer plus all clues, and the solutions are permuted cyclic Latin squares. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `pianoroll`: piano roll: a 24-semitone keyboard strip on the left (white keys bright rows, black keys dark rows) and a 24-row × 32-step grid on the right with a melody that is a random walk in a seed-chosen major scale starting on its tonic (one or two scale degrees at a time, no repeated pitch, notes of 1 to 4 steps). No musical rhythm or harmony, no modulation or rests. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `binaryclock`: binary clock: 2×2 BCD clock faces per tile showing four consecutive seconds in reading order (six columns × four dots, bits 8, 4, 2, 1 from top to bottom, bright green = 1), with the start time picked by the seed anywhere in a day. 24-hour clock only; the carry logic is not covered by the tested seeds. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `othelloboard`: Othello board: 2×2 green 8×8 boards per tile showing, in reading order, four consecutive positions of a random Othello game, each one legal move (with its flips) after the previous one. The game is random legal moves, not a meaningful match. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `goboards`: Go boards: 2×2 wood-grained 9×9 boards per tile showing positions of random legal games after 16, 28, 42 and 58 moves (random points, with captures and the no-suicide rule; no ko, passing or end of game). The games are random, not meaningful, and white stones are cream. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `dewbeads`: dew drops: spherical-cap drops (12×12 cells, about 62% occupied, radius 0.014 to 0.028 of the tile, never touching) on a dark green leaf with slanting veins and mottling; bright centre, dark rim, a highlight at the upper left, smoother than the leaf. Simple caps with positions tied to the cells. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.3 m nominal tile, 0.008 UV-relative relief.
- `bowlingscoresheet`: bowling score sheet: four games of ten frames per tile, each frame's top right holding the roll results (X strike, / spare, - miss, otherwise pins; three boxes in frame 10) with the cumulative score in dot-matrix digits below, by the standard scoring rules. The rolls are random (about 30% strikes and 30% spares) and there are only four games. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.003 UV-relative relief.
- `hexresourceboard`: hexagonal resource island: a 19-hexagon island (3-4-5-4-3) in the middle of each tile with six brightness levels for six terrains (4, 3, 4, 4, 3, 1 hexagons), each of the 18 non-desert hexagons carrying a number token from 2 to 12 with 6 and 8 never adjacent (the Catan rule). Terrain is told apart only by brightness and the rules are from memory. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.005 UV-relative relief.
- `kakuropuzzle`: Kakuro puzzle: a solved 8×8 Kakuro grid per tile — black first row and column plus 6 to 9 random black cells (every white run at least two long), white cells filled with 1 to 9 with no repeats in any run, black cells split by a diagonal carrying the across sum (top right) and down sum (bottom left). It shows the answer and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `hashibridges`: bridge puzzle (Hashiwokakero): a solved puzzle per tile on a 9×9 lattice — round islands numbered 1 to 8 joined by horizontal or vertical single or double bridges, each island's number equal to its bridge count, no crossings, all connected. It shows the answer, does not guarantee a unique solution, and has about 15 islands. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `slitherlinkloop`: Slitherlink: a solved 7×7 board per tile with small lattice dots, one thick closed loop along the lattice lines (the outline of a connected region) and in each cell the number of its sides that belong to the loop (0 to 3). It shows the answer and all numbers and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `masyupearls`: Masyu: a solved puzzle per tile — a closed loop along the lattice with white pearls (straight with at least one turning neighbour) and black pearls (a turn whose two arms go straight through the next dot), placed only at dots that satisfy the rules (at least 5). It shows the answer, the rules are from memory and a unique solution is not guaranteed. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `stringartcurves`: string art: 2×2 circles per tile, each with 96 points and chords joining point i to (k·i mod 96) for k = 2, 3, 4 and 5 (quadrants shuffled by the seed); the envelopes are a cardioid, a nephroid and curves with more lobes. Lines add up; the sampler is slower (about 223 ms for 512²). M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.003 UV-relative relief.
- `fillominoregions`: Fillomino: a solved 9×9 board per tile where every cell shows the size of its region (equal numbers inside a region, with as many cells as the number) and regions are separated by thick lines; touching regions always differ in size. It shows the answer and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `futoshikigrid`: Futoshiki: a solved 5×5 board per tile with dot-matrix digits 1 to 5 (a Latin square) and inequality signs drawn between about 55% of neighbouring cells ("<" / ">" horizontally, "∧" / "∨" vertically, apex towards the smaller number). It shows the answer and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `nurikabeislands`: Nurikabe: a solved 8×8 board per tile; light islands carry a number on one cell equal to their size and never touch, and the deep-blue sea is one connected body with no 2×2 pool. It shows the answer and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `tentsandtrees`: Tents puzzle: a solved 8×8 board per tile where every tent pairs one-to-one with an orthogonally adjacent tree, tents never touch even diagonally, and dot-matrix digits on the right and below give each row's and column's tent count. It shows the answer and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `shikakurects`: Shikaku: a solved 10×10 board per tile cut by thick lines into rectangles, each holding exactly one dot-matrix number equal to its cell count (at most 9). It shows the answer and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `hitorishading`: Hitori: a solved 7×7 board per tile where blacked-out cells (dark with reversed digits) never touch orthogonally, the rest stay connected with no repeated number per row or column, and every blacked-out number repeats an unblacked one in its row or column. It shows the answer and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `akarilights`: Akari (Light Up): a solved 8×8 board per tile where bulbs light their row and column up to a wall, every white cell is lit, no two bulbs see each other, and some walls carry a reversed dot-matrix number (0 to 4) giving their adjacent bulb count. It shows the answer and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `takuzugrid`: Takuzu (Binairo): a solved 8×8 board per tile of discs and rings with four of each per row and column, no three equal in a line, and no two identical rows or columns. It shows the answer and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `kenkencages`: KenKen: a solved 4×4 board per tile with large dot-matrix digits 1 to 4 (a Latin square), cut by thick lines into cages of 1 to 3 cells whose top-left corner carries a small dot-matrix target and operator (+, ×, −, ÷; a single cell shows just the number). It shows the answer and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `numberlinkpaths`: Numberlink: a solved 6×6 board per tile with about 6 to 9 pairs of disc endpoints (reversed dot-matrix numbers 1 to 9), each pair joined by a thick line, the paths together filling all 36 cells without crossing, branching or loops. It shows the answer and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `rushhourjam`: Rush Hour: a 6×6 sliding-vehicle board per tile with a dark two-cell target car in the exit row and about ten horizontal or vertical vehicles of 2 to 3 cells (grey rounded rectangles), a wall around the board that is open in the exit row on the right, and no overlapping vehicles. It is just one board state and is not guaranteed solvable. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `mancalaboard`: Mancala: a wooden board per tile with an elongated store at each end and two rows of six round pits holding 48 pale stones distributed at random (laid out from the centre outwards). It is not a legal game state. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.006 UV-relative relief.
- `hidatochain`: Hidato: a solved 7×7 board per tile where 1 to 49 each appear once and consecutive numbers k and k+1 are king-move neighbours, forming a path through every cell; the start 1 and end 49 are drawn dark with reversed digits. It shows every answer and does not guarantee a unique solution. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `chinesecheckersboard`: Chinese checkers: a wooden star board per tile with a six-pointed star of 121 round holes and ten marbles each of six brightness levels (one per point to start, then 14 randomly moved to empty holes). The six kinds differ by brightness only and the moves are not legal jumps. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.006 UV-relative relief.
- `colormapregions`: map colouring: each tile is a seamlessly tiling periodic Voronoi map (36 regions, thin dark borders) where neighbouring regions (including across the tile edge) never share a brightness; four brightnesses are tried first and five when needed (66 of 100 measured seeds needed only four). Four colours are not guaranteed to suffice on a torus. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `draughtsposition`: Draughts (checkers): an 8×8 board per tile with pieces only on the dark squares, 6 to 12 per side (pale and charcoal discs), about a fifth being kings with an opposite-tone inner ring. Random positions, not a legal game. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.006 UV-relative relief.
- `multiplicationtable`: times table: a 9×9 multiplication table per tile with the factors 1 to 9 as reversed digits on a dark first row and column and the 81 products 1 to 81 inside, the diagonal squares on a slightly darker background. The content is fixed and the seed only affects paper noise. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `moonphases`: moon-phase calendar: 30 moons (5 by 6) per tile for 30 consecutive days of a 29.53-day synodic month, waxing lit from the right and waning from the left with an elliptical terminator, lit share (1−cos 2πφ)/2, and the same craters on every moon. Not an ephemeris. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `treemaprects`: treemap: a square per tile cut into 12 rectangles whose areas are proportional to their values (4 to 30, written in 3×5 dot-matrix digits in the top-left corner), larger values lighter, laid out by balanced binary recursion. Random values, not squarified. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `analogclocks`: analogue clocks: a 4×4 wall of 16 clocks per tile, each showing a random time; the minute hand turns 6° per minute and the hour hand 30° per hour plus 0.5° per minute, the hour hand shorter and thicker. No second hand or numerals. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.005 UV-relative relief.
- `piechartwedges`: pie chart: an 8-wedge pie per tile whose central angles are their percentages × 3.6° (each at least 4%, adding up to 100%, written in 3×5 dot-matrix digits mid-wedge), brightness by size rank, wedges separated by thin dark lines. Random percentages. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `regressionscatter`: regression scatter plot: a scatter plot per tile with up to 28 dots round a line of random slope (approximately normal noise, no two dots close together) and the least-squares line of best fit drawn across the plot, with light grid lines and a thin frame. No tick numerals. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `barchartbars`: bar chart: 10 bars side by side per tile, each a whole number of units high (1 to 9, a unit being 0.075 of the tile height) with its value in dot-matrix digits above it, faint horizontal rules at every whole height, larger values lighter. Random values, no tick numerals. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `truthtablelogic`: truth table: a four-input Boolean truth table per tile with header A, B, C, D and output X and 16 rows counting ABCD in binary beside X (cells where X is 1 have a darker background); the output is one of eight fixed functions chosen by the seed (including a multiplexer, majority and parity). No simplified expression. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `fouriersquarewaves`: Fourier square waves: six stacked panels per tile, panel n drawing the partial sum of the first n odd harmonics of the square wave's Fourier series, (4/π)Σ sin(kt)/k, showing the ripple and the Gibbs overshoot, with an axis, the ideal square wave's reference levels and 2 periods across. The content is fixed and the seed only affects paper noise. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `pascaltrianglenumbers`: Pascal triangle numbers: the first 10 rows of Pascal's triangle per tile in dot-matrix digits laid out as a centred triangle, each entry the sum of the two above it; odd entries sit on a darker disc, tracing the Sierpinski pattern. The content is fixed and the seed only affects paper noise. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `convexhullpoints`: convex hull: a scatter of up to 16 points per tile (spacing and edge margin keep it seamless) with their convex hull: hull vertices as larger dots, inner points as smaller dots, the polygon filled light grey and outlined; the point set is chosen to avoid nearly collinear vertices and inner points hugging an edge. No algorithm steps. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `delaunaytriangles`: Delaunay triangulation: a scatter of up to 18 points per tile (spacing and edge margin keep it seamless) joined into their Delaunay triangulation, found by brute force (no other point inside a triple's circumcircle), with softly shaded light-grey triangles, thin edge lines and dark vertices. No circumcircles; near-cocircular ties are not specially handled; about 0.4 s for one 512×512 render (Bun). M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `collatzsequences`: Collatz sequences: 10 rows per tile, each the Collatz sequence of a start value (halve an even number, otherwise triple and add one, until 1) in dot-matrix digits one per cell with odd cells darker; the seed picks 10 different start values out of the 21 whose sequences have at most 13 terms. No long trajectories. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `hammingcodewords`: Hamming code words: a Hamming(7,4) table per tile: 16 rows (data 0 to 15) of 7-bit code words (parity bits at positions 1, 2 and 4), each row with one bit flipped or not as chosen by the seed (shaded dark), and a rightmost syndrome column S that names the flipped position exactly (0 for none). Single-bit errors only. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `cayleytables`: Cayley tables: the multiplication table of a group of order 8 per tile (9×9 with headers, identity cells darker); the seed picks one of Z8, Z4×Z2, Z2³, D4 and Q8 and shuffles the labels of elements 1 to 7. It satisfies the group axioms; no group name shown. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `romannumerals`: Roman numerals: 14 rows per tile, each a decimal number (1 to 199), an equals sign and its standard subtractive Roman numeral (dot-matrix I, V, X, L and C, e.g. IV, XC, CLXXXVIII); the numbers are picked by the seed, all different and ascending. No D, M or variants such as IIII. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `signedencodings`: signed integer encodings: a 4-bit lookup table per tile: 16 rows of bits (the sign-bit column darkest), the unsigned value and the signed value; the seed picks one of two's complement, one's complement and sign-magnitude, with negative rows slightly darker (one's complement and sign-magnitude have a "−0"). 4 bits only. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `matrixproducts`: matrix products: 4 equations A × B = C of 3×3 matrices per tile (A and B single digits 0 to 9 drawn by the seed, C their product with up to three digits), with square brackets and the product cells slightly darker. No working shown. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `kochsnowflake`: Koch snowflake: four 2×2 panels per tile showing the snowflake after 0, 1, 2 and 3 iterations (an equilateral triangle whose edges' middle thirds become outward bumps); panel order and rotation are drawn by the seed, filled by the even-odd rule with a dark outline. The boundary grows by 4/3 per iteration and the area follows A₀(8/5−(3/5)(4/9)ⁿ). Up to 3 iterations only. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `newtonbasins`: Newton basins: per tile the basins of attraction of Newton's method for z^n − 1 (n from 3 to 5, chosen by the seed) in the complex window [−1.6, 1.6]², painted by the root reached with slow convergence slightly darker, the coordinates folded by a triangle wave so the edge is continuous. Brightness only encodes root and speed, not hue; pixels near the boundary are sensitive to floating-point error. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `mandelbrotbands`: Mandelbrot bands: a smooth escape-time contour picture of the Mandelbrot set per tile; the seed picks one of four fixed windows (the whole set, the seahorse valley, a branch near the imaginary axis, the neighbourhood of the Feigenbaum point), the inside dark and the outside shaded by ν = n + 1 − log₂(log|z|/log 256) with a cosine contour band, the coordinates folded by a triangle wave so the edge is continuous. Brightness only encodes escape speed; at most 160 iterations per pixel. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `cantorsetrows`: Cantor set rows: 8 horizontal bands per tile from top to bottom, the k-th the set after k steps of the Cantor construction (the middle part of every interval removed each step); the seed picks the fraction removed (a third, a fifth or a half), and band k has 2^k equal intervals of total length (1 − r)^k. 8 steps only. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.8 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `conicsections`: conic sections: four 2×2 panels per tile showing a circle, an ellipse, a parabola and a hyperbola (both branches) with eccentricity 0, 0.6, 1 and 1.6, drawn from "distance to the focus = e × distance to the directrix" with the focus and the directrix (dashed) marked; the arrangement is chosen by the seed. The axis is fixed horizontal. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `rosecurves`: rose curves: nine 3×3 panels per tile each drawing r = R·cos(kθ) (k = 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 3/2, 5/2, 5/3, 7/3), the arrangement chosen by the seed; lines drawn using a first-order distance estimate to the polar implicit function with the origin marked. No formula labels, no rotation phase. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `twosourceripples`: two-source ripples: per tile the interference pattern of two equal circular wave sources (horizontally 0.22 or 0.32 UV apart, wavelength 0.04 / 0.052 / 0.07 UV, picked by the seed), brightness cos(k·r₁) + cos(k·r₂), with the nodal lines on the hyperbolas where r₁−r₂ is an odd multiple of half a wavelength, the coordinates folded by a triangle wave so the edge is continuous. No attenuation or reflecting boundary. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.006 UV-relative relief.
- `luhnnumbers`: Luhn numbers: 10 rows of 16-digit numbers per tile (in groups of four) whose last digit is the check digit (darker cell), each row ending with a check mark if it passes the Luhn test and a cross if it does not (about 60% valid, the rest with one digit changed). The digits are random, not real card numbers. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `bezierconstruction`: Bezier construction: four 2×2 panels per tile each drawing a cubic Bezier curve with fixed control points (four different shapes, arranged by the seed) with de Casteljau's interpolation segments at a parameter t (1/4, 1/3, 1/2 or 2/3, picked by the seed): control polygon dashed, end points as squares, inner control points as rings and the point on the curve as a filled dot. The curve is a 64-segment polyline approximation. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `vennregions`: Venn regions: a three-set Venn diagram per tile (three equal circles, each of the seven regions labelled with an element count from 0 to 40, overlaps shaded darker) with the three totals for A, B and C (the sum of the regions inside each circle) listed at the top left. The counts are random, not real data. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `ieee754floats`: IEEE 754 single-precision floats: six numbers per tile, each a line with the decimal value (4 decimal places) above its 32 bits (sign, 8 exponent and 23 mantissa fields on different backgrounds), the value corresponding to the bits by (−1)^s × 2^(e−127) × (1+m/2²³); the exponent ranges over 124 to 133. Normal numbers only. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `bellcurvebands`: bell-curve bands: a normal-distribution bell curve per tile with the area under it split into four brightness bands by the number of standard deviations from the mean (within 1σ, 1 to 2σ, 2 to 3σ, beyond 3σ) and a tick at each σ; σ is picked by the seed from 0.07, 0.09 and 0.11 UV and the height is fixed. The band areas follow the 68–95–99.7 rule. No axis labels. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `roundingmodes`: rounding modes: a comparison table per tile: 12 values of x (from −3.5 to 6.5, picked by the seed from a pool of 20, ascending) with five roundings — FL (floor), CE (ceiling), TR (toward zero), RN (halves away from zero) and RE (halves to even); in rows where x is exactly a half the RN and RE columns are darker. A zero result has no minus sign. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `binaryaddition`: binary addition: three column additions per tile, each adding two random 8-bit numbers: small carry digits above the columns that receive them, then A, B with a "+" sign, a rule and the 9-bit sum (darker sum row); every column obeys the full-adder rule. The numbers come from the seed. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `abeliansandpile`: abelian sandpile: the stable sandpile left after 4000, 5200 or 6400 grains (picked by the seed) are dropped on the centre of an 81 × 81 grid (a cell with more than 3 grains gives to its four neighbours), painted in four brightnesses for 0 to 3 grains per cell, with the eightfold symmetry of the square and a 0-grain background outside the pile. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `bitwiselogic`: bitwise logic: four blocks per tile, each applying AND, OR, XOR or NAND bit by bit to two random 8-bit numbers A and B (each once, in a seed-determined order), with the operation name at the left of the B row and the result row below the rule, whose 1 bits have a darker background. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `burningship`: Burning Ship fractal: smooth escape-time contours of the Burning Ship fractal (z → (|Re z| + i|Im z|)² + c) per tile with non-escaping points darkest; the view is picked by the seed from four preset regions and the coordinates are folded so the picture is continuous across tile edges and mirrored. No axis labels. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `modularpowers`: modular powers: a 10 × 10 table per tile with exponent k = 1 to 10 and base a = 2 to 11, the body printing the remainder of a^k divided by m (m picked by the seed from 13, 15, 17, 21 and 25), cells equal to 1 darker and whole rows of bases sharing a factor with m pale grey. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `primefactors`: prime factors: 12 rows per tile, each with an integer from 2 to 99 on the left (12 distinct values picked by the seed, ascending) and its prime factorization on the right (smallest first, joined by ×, for example 2×2×3×7); rows whose number is prime have a darker background. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `baseconversion`: base conversion: 12 rows per tile, each an integer from 0 to 255 (12 distinct values picked by the seed, ascending) in decimal, 8-digit binary, 3-digit octal and 2-digit hexadecimal (A to F as capital letters); alternating row backgrounds and darker hexadecimal cells containing A to F. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `caesarcipher`: Caesar cipher: three groups per tile: the alphabet A to M, N to Z and a short message, each with its shifted cipher row below (pale grey); the shift is picked by the seed from 3, 5, 7 and 13 and the message from five preset phrases. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `wordsquares`: symmetric word squares: a word square per tile in which row i equals column i, picked by the seed from six presets (the 5 × 5 SATOR square and five 4 × 4 English squares) with the diagonal cells darker. The words were found from a hand-written list and not checked against a dictionary. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `vectorfieldarrows`: vector field arrows: an arrow at each point of a 10 × 10 grid per tile pointing along a vector field, with length and darkness growing with the size of the vector; the field is picked by the seed from four presets: rotation, saddle, source and spiral. No numeric labels. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `mobiusgrid`: Moebius grid: a checkered square grid per tile after a Moebius transformation w = (az + b)/(cz + d) (picked by the seed from an inversion, an elliptic and a loxodromic preset), with grid lines becoming circles or lines, the checkerboard shading carried along and a line width uniform on screen; coordinates are folded so the picture is continuous across tile edges and mirrored. No pole markings. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `dipolefieldlines`: dipole field lines: equipotentials (potential V = ln(r₂/r₁) every 0.4) and field lines (angle difference every π/10) of a pair of opposite two-dimensional line charges (separation picked by the seed from three presets) per tile, with different backgrounds for positive and negative potential, small round cores at the charges and a line width uniform on screen; coordinates are folded so the picture is continuous across tile edges and mirrored. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `numberwords`: numbers spelled out in English: 12 rows per tile, each with an integer from 0 to 99 on the left (12 distinct values picked by the seed, ascending) and its English name on the right (such as FORTY TWO); alternating row backgrounds and darker number cells below 20. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `sorobanabacus`: soroban abacus: two seven-rod Japanese abacuses per tile (one heaven bead above the beam and four earth beads below it), beads against the beam counting, each rod showing a digit from 0 to 9; the seven-digit number it shows (chosen by the seed) is printed below in dot-matrix digits. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `boxplots`: box plots: three box plots per tile, each group's 15 values drawn as dots on two alternating rows with the box from Q1 to Q3, the median line and whiskers out to the minimum and maximum above them (quartiles by Tukey's hinges, no interpolation); the three groups lean differently. No axis labels. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `stemandleaf`: stem-and-leaf plot: a plot of 29 integers from 10 to 99 per tile (tens as stems, ones as leaves, ascending) with the leaf of the 15th sorted value (the median) on a darker background; values are the mean of two random numbers, so the middle stems have more leaves. No key. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `substitutionwords`: substitution words: rows of words over 0 and 1 stacked per tile, the first row "0" and each later row the previous one with every letter replaced by a rule; 1 is a dark cell and 0 a mid-grey cell, all rows sharing one cell width and left aligned. The seed picks the rule from the Fibonacci word (0→01, 1→0), Thue–Morse (0→01, 1→10) and period doubling (0→01, 1→00). No markings. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `dicesumbars`: dice sum bars: the distribution of the sum of two n-sided dice (n picked by the seed from 4, 6 and 8) per tile, one column per sum (2 to 2n, printed below) with a stack of squares counting the ordered pairs of faces that make that sum (the likeliest column darker). M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `binaryheaptree`: binary heap tree: a complete binary tree of 4 levels and 15 nodes per tile, each node a circle with a two-digit number from 10 to 99 and parents joined to children by lines; the values satisfy the min-heap (or max-heap) property (decided by the seed, built by insert and sift-up) and the root is darker. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `bubblesortpasses`: bubble sort passes: an 8 × 8 table per tile whose first row is a random order of 1 to 8 (decided by the seed) and each later row the result of one more bubble-sort pass (7 passes), with the last k cells of row k (already in their final places) on a darker background and the last row the sorted 1 to 8. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `graphcoloring`: graph colouring: a graph per tile (the cube, the Petersen graph or a five-spoke wheel, picked by the seed) whose vertices are filled with brightnesses by colour class so that no edge joins two equal brightnesses, using as many colours as the graph's chromatic number (2, 3 or 4). No vertex numbers. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `runlengthrows`: run-length rows: five groups per tile, each a row of 20 black and white cells (alternating random runs 2 to 6 long) with its run-length code written below in dot-matrix characters (each run's length plus W or B, for example 5W4B6W4B1W). M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `isbn10checks`: ISBN-10 check digits: 11 random ISBN-10 numbers (g-ppp-ttttt-c) per tile, the first 9 digits decided by the seed and the last (on a darker background) the check digit that makes 10·d₁ + 9·d₂ + … + 1·d₁₀ a multiple of 11 (10 is written X); alternating row backgrounds. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `projectilearcs`: projectile arcs: two fountains repeated vertically per tile, with five parabolas at the same speed and launch angles 15° to 75° flying to both sides from the middle line and landing on a ground line, plus a fainter safety parabola touching every path; the maximum range is one of three picked by the seed. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `sternbrocottree`: binary tree of fractions: four levels (1, 2, 4 and 8 fractions) per tile written in dot-matrix characters and linked to their children by straight lines; the seed picks a Stern–Brocot or Calkin–Wilf tree. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `cycloidcusps`: cycloid cusps: 3 × 3 cells per tile, each with an epicycloid or hypocycloid with k cusps and a pale grey fixed circle (row 1 epicycloids k = 1, 2, 3; row 2 hypocycloids k = 3, 4, 5; row 3 picked by the seed: epicycloids k = 4, 5, 6 or hypocycloids k = 6, 7, 8); the seed also picks one of four rotations. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.9 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `zeckendorfrows`: Zeckendorf representation rows: two columns of 12 rows, 24 consecutive integers per tile, each row giving the decimal number and its eight-digit Zeckendorf representation (Fibonacci place values 34, 21, 13, 8, 5, 3, 2, 1, never two neighbouring 1s); the seed picks the first number from 1 to 31, and odd rows have a faint band. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `ferrersdiagrams`: Ferrers diagrams: a 5 × 3 grid per tile showing the 15 integer partitions of 7 as rows of boxes (left aligned, each diagram centred) in reverse lexicographic order; the seed picks the English or French style and whether the diagrams are transposed. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.7 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `pairingtables`: pairing-function tables: an 8 × 8 table per tile with each cell writing the natural number that pairs its column x and row y, on a faint checkerboard background; the seed picks the Cantor (diagonals), Szudzik (square shells) or Morton (Z-order bit interleaving) rule and whether x and y are swapped. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.8 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `hexdumprows`: hex dump rows: 14 rows of random bytes per tile, each a two-digit hex offset, six bytes and the matching text (capital letters, digits and spaces, with about 14% control codes shown as "."); the offset grows by 6 per row from a seed-picked start, and odd rows have a faint band. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.6 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `pythagoreantriples`: Pythagorean triples: 12 primitive triples per tile, each row giving Euclid's m and n and a = m² − n², b = 2mn, c = m² + n² (the c column on a darker background); m is at most 9, which gives 18 pairs, and the seed picks 12 and sorts them by hypotenuse. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `figuratenumbers`: figurate-number dot patterns: 4 rows × 5 columns per tile (n = 1 to 5), each row one kind (triangular, square, oblong n × (n+1) and centred square diamond), each figure centred in its cell; the seed shuffles the order of the four kinds from top to bottom, and alternate rows have a faint band. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.8 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `sierpinskipascal`: Pascal-parity triangles: the whole tile is a 2^d × 2^d grid (d = 5, 6 or 7 picked by the seed) whose cell in column i and row j is dark when C(i + j, i) is odd (the Sierpiński triangle), plus the same picture turned half a turn; the seed also decides left-right mirroring. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.8 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `cobwebplots`: cobweb plots: the whole tile draws a cobweb plot of the logistic map f(x) = r·x·(1 − x) with a thin square frame, the grey parabola and diagonal and the dark cobweb (14 iterations); the seed picks one of 5 values of r and 4 of x₀ (20 combinations). M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.8 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `divisorcountbars`: divisor-count bars: the whole tile is a bar chart of 64 consecutive integers (the start picked by the seed from 1 to 400), each bar a stack of small boxes with one box per divisor (so its height is d(n)), with primes darkest and perfect squares lightest and a grey baseline underneath. M0, mortar 0; defaults 1 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `timestablecircles`: times-table circles: a 3 × 3 grid per tile, each cell a circle with 72 equally spaced points joined by thin lines from point i to point k·i (modulo 72), the nine multipliers k taken by the seed from 2 to 20 by a shuffle (all different), plus a grey ring, dark dots and a checkerboard background. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.9 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `josephuscircle`: Josephus circle: the whole tile shows n people in a circle with every k-th one eliminated, each disc showing the order in which that person leaves, the survivor on a darker grey disc and k written large in the middle; the seed picks n (17, 19, 21, 23 or 24) and k (2 to 5). M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.9 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `periodictablecells`: periodic-table cells: the whole tile draws the layout of the first four periods (elements 1 to 36) in 18 columns and 4 rows, each cell with the atomic number above and a capital-letter symbol below and blanks where the table has no element; the seed decides whether the three background shades mean block, metal / metalloid / nonmetal, or solid / liquid / gas at 25 °C. M0, mortar 0; defaults 1 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `circleoffifths`: circle of fifths: the whole tile is a circle of fifths with the 12 major keys on the outer ring (each a perfect fifth above the previous one), their relative minors in the middle and the number of sharps or flats inside, on 12 alternating sectors with three thin rings; the seed picks the key at the top (12 choices) and whether the fifths run clockwise or anticlockwise. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.9 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `roulettewheel`: roulette wheel: the whole tile is the ring of the 37 pockets of a European wheel in the actual wheel order, each showing its number, red pockets mid grey, black pockets dark and the zero light, with a grey disc in the middle; the seed picks the pocket at the top (37 choices) and the reading direction. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.9 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
- `roundrobintable`: round-robin schedule: the whole tile is the schedule of n teams (8 or 10 picked by the seed) by the circle method, n − 1 rounds each with its matches on one row, every team playing once per round and every pair meeting exactly once; the team numbering is shuffled by the seed and odd rows have a faint band. M0, mortar 0; defaults 0.8 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief.
